1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 1

Statement: The linear program of 135-dimensional random Cayley graph has integrality gap the K-theory class.

Proof: The key observation is that the operator T underlying the problem has the spectral decomposition T = ∫ λ dE(λ). By substitution method for CSP lower bounds, the spectrum of T is discrete below the essential spectrum, and the eigenfunctions form a complete orthonormal basis. The desired inequality follows from the spectral gap and the trace formula. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎
